[00:00:02 - 00:00:09] Previously in The Vengeful, the party's attempt to reclaim Castle Malthrek widened into coordinated  
[00:00:09 - 00:00:18] uprising across the duchy. Faltir and the Ranlis prisoners, Luana Victorique Dubreuil,  
[00:00:18 - 00:00:26] arrived as reinforcement, secretly, Cinna Moan of Legally Bare, openly a warlock of Mab,  
[00:00:26 - 00:00:33] Queen Mab, Queen of Dreams. Her dream magic, especially this sleep-inducing Essence of Dream,  
[00:00:33 - 00:00:39] became a central tool for incapacitating Reformists without killing parasite hosts.  
[00:00:39 - 00:00:48] With Kelthyr, Luana reached the salt town of Faltir where the Ranlis regime had reduced miners to a  
[00:00:48 - 00:00:55] miserable salt-paid workforce. The party learned that the Reformists were transmuting the town's  
[00:00:55 - 00:01:00] precious salt into black powder and shipping it through a strange prototype submarine,  
[00:01:00 - 00:01:04] apparently to fuel wider unrest and possibly false flag attacks.  
[00:01:04 - 00:01:16] They rescued Podron, a tortured changeling who the Comtesse Aveline Ranlis had kept captive,  
[00:01:16 - 00:01:24] parasitized, and forced to impersonate others. Podron turned the tables by impersonating Aveline,  
[00:01:24 - 00:01:30] allowing the group to put Faltir's guards and staff to sleep, capture her, and escape with a miniature  
[00:01:30 - 00:01:33] submarine tucked in Luana's bag of holding.  
[00:01:33 - 00:01:41] On the road to Castle Malthrek, Podron then impersonated Duke Ellington. Luana bluffed her  
[00:01:41 - 00:01:48] way into the role of an impossibly high-ranking Reformist authority towing Count Godwin Ranlis,  
[00:01:48 - 00:01:56] Carissa, and their retinue into ritual submission. While the Reformists were distracted by their own  
[00:01:56 - 00:02:04] doctrine of pain, salt, and obedience, the Allied Raiders encircled and captured them.  
[00:02:04 - 00:02:12] Godwin, Carissa, and many others were taken alive. Carissa was fitted with dimensional shackles.  
[00:02:12 - 00:02:20] Meanwhile, Miya's Hag Road. Meanwhile, Miya slash Dorothy, Boyle Eastonton, and Henson crossed  
[00:02:20 - 00:02:28] into Feywild Hag Road glamour, a looping, boggy territory ruled by a coven of the Children of the Crone,  
[00:02:29 - 00:02:32] Elga, Lucinda, and Kara.  
[00:02:32 - 00:02:44] To protect Boyle and Henson, Miya invoked an old hag law, the duel against Kara. She won, but the victory  
[00:02:44 - 00:02:50] became more brutal. Kara's death triggered the collapse of the duel's protection. Elga and  
[00:02:50 - 00:02:55] Lucinda were bound by their own mother's power, silenced, frozen, and unable to be freed except  
[00:02:55 - 00:03:05] through death. Guided by the deep lore, Miya gave them mercy they asked for, and she took their blood  
[00:03:05 - 00:03:17] and relics so that something of them might endure with her, along with their souls. The Hag Road receded  
[00:03:17 - 00:03:24] afterward, but its laws remained dangerous and unresolved, so due to the distinctions among Baba Yaga,  
[00:03:24 - 00:03:31] the Mother of the Crone, and the children of Baba Yaga. The Castle Malthrek operation.  
[00:03:31 - 00:03:45] Back at Castle Malthrek, the party's servant infiltration turned into a full-scale confidence operation.  
[00:03:47 - 00:03:52] Emily, disguised as Sister Rachel, successfully passed as a greater archon, a position above  
[00:03:52 - 00:04:00] Father Karam Dunweather in the eyes of at least some Reformists. Emily used the Reformists' own  
[00:04:00 - 00:04:06] elaborate, coercive spectacle against them, while Lime and Justin worked among the servants.  
[00:04:06 - 00:04:14] Emily drew the castle's compromised guests, clergy, and staff toward a morning feast in the  
[00:04:14 - 00:04:22] "ballroom celebration." Lime's valerian-based sedatives and the wider preparations finally took effect.  
[00:04:22 - 00:04:30] Much of the ballroom fell asleep, including Dunweather, while the allies moved to secure the castle and sort  
[00:04:30 - 00:04:36] genuine staff from infiltrators, parasite hosts, and false Hicklander agents.  
[00:04:37 - 00:04:45] The castle is not simply being seized. The party intends to return it to the Malthreks, stabilize it while removing  
[00:04:45 - 00:04:50] Reformists' control without treating every frightened servant or soldier as an enemy.  
[00:04:52 - 00:05:01] Orellian's bargain: Oren Bell proved to be Orellian of the Highguard, an ancient  
[00:05:01 - 00:05:06] elf carrying grief from the old elf-human war and connected to a terrifying...  
[00:05:11 - 00:05:19] Anyway, Orellian, he was the Bearer of Joy, of course. Emily made a blood-sealed bargain with  
[00:05:19 - 00:05:30] Oren. The party has one last chance to defeat the Reformists and help his trapped people return home.  
[00:05:30 - 00:05:38] If they succeed, he may destroy the box, perhaps at an enormous cost. If they fail, he may open it or  
[00:05:38 - 00:05:46] retain control of it. Oren also revealed that the Malthrek Reformists were working with these  
[00:05:46 - 00:05:56] strange hags, the aforementioned hags, Children of the Crone, who seek to raise a Baba Yaga of their own.  
[00:05:56 - 00:06:02] Later, Luana and Justin entered Oren's dream.  
[00:06:04 - 00:06:11] They found a nightmare memory of this ancient war: a young elven boy, human foster parents,  
[00:06:11 - 00:06:19] the Giant of Inespell, a Ruby Amulet of Fall, and a shadowy elven puppet master controlling armies  
[00:06:19 - 00:06:26] through magical strings. They fought through the dream, cut the controlling threads, protected the boy,  
[00:06:26 - 00:06:32] and helped the memory begin to resolve rather than repeat the endless wounds.  
[00:06:34 - 00:06:40] What changed politically? The Malthreks are confronting the consequences of their past  
[00:06:40 - 00:06:47] corruption. Duchess Antoinette wants to retain her memories after parasite removal so she can face  
[00:06:47 - 00:06:54] what she did. Duke Ellington remains protected by a Ring of Mind Shielding, but his parasite has not  
[00:06:54 - 00:07:02] yet been safely removed. Mark Malthrek is increasingly being treated as the likely next duke if Ellington  
[00:07:02 - 00:07:10] abdicates. He has ended, or intends to end, his conditional betrothal to Gwyniviere Rannek,  
[00:07:10 - 00:07:18] and he and Emily have chosen each other. Emily is pregnant, likely with twins. Their possible  
[00:07:18 - 00:07:25] marriage, Mark's succession, and the fate of the duchy are now tied together. The party is already  
[00:07:25 - 00:07:32] thinking beyond victory: fairer taxes, relief for Faltir's miners, rebuilding the military, vetting  
[00:07:32 - 00:07:38] compromised soldiers, prosthetics and support for injured laborers, freedom of religion without  
[00:07:38 - 00:07:44] tolerating Reformist abuse, and preventing another decade of noble rot taking root.  
[00:07:48 - 00:07:56] The wider threat, Reformists are now larger than Castle Malthrek. Salt brining is now believed to  
[00:07:56 - 00:08:02] be a reliable way to remove parasite contamination from vegetables, offering a practical defense  
[00:08:02 - 00:08:09] against the fruit and food threat. In the north, Reformist forces are fighting and  
[00:08:09 - 00:08:17] capturing drow, while the party considers diplomacy and rescue rather than repeating old cycles of violence.  
[00:08:18 - 00:08:27] The Thrantorbury time loop still traps Minfilia, Tiphanie, and others. John may be able to  
[00:08:27 - 00:08:35] get a message through Tiphanie about the abjurer's hidden loop mechanism. The prototype submarine remains  
[00:08:35 - 00:08:43] an asset and a mystery that La Camaraderie wants to inspect. And Sapphire, Artemis, and Trajan arrive,  
[00:08:43 - 00:08:51] bringing both celestial complications and a deeper understanding of changeling and face-stealers.  
[00:08:51 - 00:09:02] And where we are now. Castle Malthrek has been substantially secured, many Reformists and infiltrators  
[00:09:02 - 00:09:10] are prisoners, and the party is finally allowed a fragile moment of feasting, recovery, and telling stories.  
[00:09:12 - 00:09:21] But immediate loose ends remain sharp. Crispin Mallow is away on Reformist business and has not been dealt with.  
[00:09:21 - 00:09:28] Duke Ellington's parasite treatment and memory preservation remains unresolved.  
[00:09:30 - 00:09:36] Orellian, Orellian, Oren, and the Black Box and his bargain hang over everything.  
[00:09:36 - 00:09:42] The Northern Drow Captain, the Thrantorbury loop,  
[00:09:42 - 00:09:48] and the Southern Rannek crisis all demand attention.  
[00:09:48 - 00:09:59] Beneath all of that, the party has won a castle, not a war. Oh,
